# ANSYS 歧管微通道网格质量



优先复用已验证的方法和脚本，按当前模型重新验收。案例库已有 **4 个独立几何模型的成功验收**，另补充同一 50 zhi 几何的[五档网格与邻域联动验证](references/five-grid-study.md)；50 juchi 的早期失败作为历史保留，最新状态以案例库为准。6 实体小单元另有直接重划成功案例，见 [小单元经验](references/small-unit.md)。不能把未验收的试验称为成功，也不能保证任意结构必定达标。



开始时读取 [案例库](references/cases.json) 和 [实际经验](references/experience.md)。执行时按需读取 [流程与工具](references/workflow.md)；遇到软件或接口问题再查 [排错记录](references/troubleshooting.md)。



## 本地约定与范围



- 保留 Workbench → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent 链路；不要自行改用 Fluent Meshing 或开始物理求解。

- 以用户当前保存的 `.wbpj` 和完整 `_files` 为准。heat 改动后，旧几何的成功结果不能沿用。目录下只有一个 `.wbpj` 时可自行解析，无需反复询问文件名。

- 默认目标是 **最低正交质量严格大于 0.07**；当前任务另有要求时采用当前目标，勿把 0.07 误写成 0.7。

- 试验和归档优先放 D 盘。先在独立副本中工作。仅当用户已授权覆盖时替换原工程，且先完整备份工程与 `_files`；不重复索要已有授权。

# ANSYS Fluent Microchannel



Use the user-required chain: **ANSYS Workbench → Geometry → ANSYS Meshing → Fluent**. Do not bypass ANSYS Meshing with Fluent Meshing unless the user explicitly changes this preference.



## Start with a simulation contract



Before solving, record:



- CAD path, ANSYS version, length unit, dimensionality, and whether the CAD contains both fluid and solid volumes.

- Which faces are `inlet`, `outlet`, `heat`, `symmetry`, and external `wall`.

- Fluid and solid materials, inlet specification, inlet temperature, outlet pressure, heat input, and intended outputs.

- Whether the case is a literal reproduction, a geometry-adapted pilot, or a production case.



Never infer physical boundaries solely from screen position. A face is an inlet/outlet only when topology, flow direction, or user confirmation supports it.



## Mandatory pre-mesh partition confirmation



Before generating or updating an ANSYS Meshing mesh, apply the geometry-partition decision process from `$ansys-partition-for-hex-mesh`. Present the proposed cuts, intended method for each resulting block, shared-topology plan, and Named Selection impact, then wait for the user's explicit confirmation. If no partition is recommended, say so and obtain confirmation to mesh the uncut geometry. A confirmation from another geometry or archived version does not carry over automatically.

from run_quality import fluent_check

from boundary_audit import snapshot,compare

from symmetry_audit import audit



if __name__=='__main__':

    tag=sys.argv[1];mode=sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else 'generated';out=R/tag

"""Validated ANSYS 2024 R2 manifold recipe. Original projects are copied first."""

import argparse

import datetime

import hashlib

import json

import re

import shutil

import subprocess

import sys

from pathlib import Path



from boundary_audit import snapshot, compare

from symmetry_audit import audit as audit_symmetry



HERE=Path(__file__).resolve().parent



def save(path,data):

    path.write_text(json.dumps(data,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')



def progress(text):

    print(text,flush=True)



def run_hidden(argv,cwd,label,timeout):

    with (cwd/(label+'.stdout.txt')).open('w',encoding='utf-8') as o, (cwd/(label+'.stderr.txt')).open('w',encoding='utf-8') as e:

        p=subprocess.Popen([str(x) for x in argv],cwd=str(cwd),stdout=o,stderr=e,

                           creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)

        try:

            rc=p.wait(timeout=timeout)

        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:

            subprocess.run(['taskkill','/PID',str(p.pid),'/T','/F'],stdout=e,stderr=e,

                           creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW)

            raise RuntimeError(label+' exceeded its timeout; only the launched process tree was stopped.')

        if rc!=0:progress(label+' launcher returned '+str(rc)+'; checking native result files.')



def quoted(path):

    return '"'+str(path).replace('\\','/')+'"'



def parse_quality(path):

    text=path.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace')

    oq=re.findall(r'Minimum Orthogonal Quality\s*=\s*([0-9.eE+\-]+)',text)

    ar=re.findall(r'Maximum Aspect Ratio\s*=\s*([0-9.eE+\-]+)',text)

    vol=re.findall(r'minimum volume \(m3\):\s*([0-9.eE+\-]+)',text)

    total=re.findall(r'total volume \(m3\):\s*([0-9.eE+\-]+)',text)

    # A transcript must close normally and contain both a completed check and quality report.

    errors=re.findall(r'^\s*Error[^\n]*',text,re.M)

    check='Checking me<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="5204" />revent_reverse_flow=True，需要报告人工壁面的实际面积并评估边界敏感性。

- G2→G3 入口区单元802744→235369、射流板933082→520950，有效三维总数加密比仅约1.050。不能据此宣称严格相似加密、GCI通过或强行让压降单调。

- 已验收的是独立原生 case/data。Workbench SolutionPoints 注册未成功，不得将其描述为原工程 Setup/Solution 已同步。



追加离线复核：五档34个流体域组成单一连通分量，33个固体均为silicon-pure，186对wall/shadow互配且API热条件为Coupled；130个symmetry区全部三角面顶点的轴向平面偏差最大约5.23e-13 m，heat面积及相邻固体保持。低于299.99 K的单元数依次334/129/227/86/24，均在流体中；G3含225个入口单元和2个微通道单元，其余档均在入口，不应误写为全部固体或全部仅在入口。



出口实际人工壁面面积依次2.72984/2.65633/2.90022/2.88483/3.20921%，封闭面的质量流率为零；开放面的面积加权静压为0 Pa，整张出口面平均为−3.242/−4.042/−3.866/−3.018/−3.483 Pa。这解释了“出口设0却平均非0”的报告现象；它给当前整面ΔP带来约3–4 Pa的直接口径差，不能据此量化关闭防回流后上游流场会改变多少。需要另做受控边界敏感性试验时再改设置，不能静默修改现有基准。



资源调度：先核对可用内存。此机同时两个8核解曾使剩余物理内存不足0.5 GB，改为串行8核。用户提出并行上限不等于任何规模都适合并行。仅关闭本任务持有的会话，验证保存后退出，归档不得带认证文件。



原理参考：[Fluent energy settings](https://ansyshelp.ansys.com/public/Views/Secured/corp/v242/en/flu_ug/flu_ug_sec_hxfer.html)、[NASA spatial convergence](https://www.grc.nasa.gov/www/wind/valid/tutorial/spatconv.html)。具体0.5/0.2/0.4来自本案例实测，不归因于官方通用推荐。

    p=out/name;tmp=p.with_suffix('.tmp');tmp.write_text(json.dumps(obj,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8');os.replace(tmp,p)

def store(status,**kw):state.update(status=status,updated=time.time(),**kw);write('state.json',state)

# 已确认的经验



截至 2026-10-15，已有四个模型完整通过验收并备份覆盖原路径，包括三个大单元和 50 juchi small danyuan：



| 模型 | 实体数 | 单元数 | 最低正交质量 | heat 实际面积 |

|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|

| 50 zhi | 67 | 2,362,695 | 0.0701507 | 0.900000 mm² |

| 30zhi | 107 | 2,076,076 | 0.0737269 | 0.900000 mm² |

| 50 juchi | 67 | 5,766,982 | 0.112796 | 0.900000 mm² |

| 50 juchi small danyuan | 6 | 895,252 | 0.200235 | 0.015000 mm² |



## 有效组合



- 50 zhi：入口和射流板 Patch Independent，最大 15 μm、最小 2.5 μm、gap=3、Delaunay 平滑过渡、增长 1.15；61 个微通道/基底实体沿 Z 轴 5 μm 扫掠。初始混合网格最低约 0.068276，8 组受约束内部节点优化后越过 0.07。

- 30zhi：入口和射流板 Patch Independent，最大 12.5 μm、最小 1.25 μm、gap=3、过渡开启、增长 1.15；其余原方法。初始混合网格最低约 0.061365，3 组内部节点优化后越过 0.07。

- 全局高平滑，控制增长；保留用户已有局部尺度。入口/射流板名称通过唯一匹配识别，不硬编码实体 ID。

- 只优化四面体而忽略金字塔，曾导致负体积。<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1113" />角或切割，但网格表面近似仍须报告。



合并同流体分区为 65 实体的保形试验虽保持体积，却没有达到目标；未采用。0.5–2 μm 恒定圆角也未产生可交付候选；未采用。一个试验因最大尺寸小于单元尺寸在生成前被拒绝，这类输入错误不应被写成网格算法对几何无效。



此扩展仅验证于平面 CAD、全四面体候选；不直接推广到曲面或混合单元。新脚本 `cad_vertex_tet_optimizer.py` 已用本例重放 6 组优化坐标，差异为 0；原有两个成功案例的混合网格脚本保持独立。





2026-10-15：50 juchi 后续已获用户授权覆盖原工程。按原工程文件名暂存后通过原生重开、独立 Fluent、边界及对称面核验；完整备份后替换，原路径再次重开复核最低正交质量仍为 0.112796，正体积及全部边界审计通过。具体备份和安装证据见 cases.json 的 replacement 项。此为原成功案例的交付更新，不新增模型计数。





## 50 juchi 六实体小单元



受控 PI（两个射流流体及射流板）+ 其余 PC 四面体直接把最低正交质量从 0.00985928 提至 0.200235，单元数从 2,741,713 降为 895,252，无需节点优化。heat 为 0.015 mm²，三组同名 symmetry 均正常，原模型无 isolate。参数、原路径重开证据及适用限制见 [小单元经验](small-unit.md)。大单元的五个角色 token 不适用于此六实体模型。





## 2026-10-16 五档网格与邻域联动



同一 50 zhi 几何完成五档原生网格保存与原路径重开验收。G2 的受约束单顶点优化停滞后，16 节点一环联动达到最低 0.0705085；CLI 重放坐标差为 0。记录了尺寸舍入反转、Workbench 输出版本以及区域单元数非单调的限制。此为同一几何的额外工作流证据，不增加独立模型数；未进行流热或网格无关性求解。详见 [五档记录](five-grid-study.md)。

---

name: skill-creator

description: Create or update a Codex skill with appropriately scoped instructions and any needed supporting resources.

metadata:

  short-description: Create or update a skill

---



# Skill Creator



Create skills that give Codex useful, non-obvious guidance without constraining unrelated work.



## Core Principles



**Assume Codex is already capable.** Include only information that changes its decisions or improves its work. Remove generic advice, repeated instructions, speculative edge cases, and examples that do not materially clarify the task.



**Preserve user intent and scope.** A skill should support the requested task, not replace the user's chosen product, expand the assignment, modify unrelated configuration, or imply permission for additional external actions. Do not turn a particular example, past failure, or personal preference into a universal requirement.



Approval to complete a task does not expand its scope or execution permissions. For retrying or externally mutating workflows, define a stopping condition proportional to the risk.



**Match specificity to the risk.** Give the model room to choose an appropriate approach when multiple approaches are reasonable. Use detailed steps, deterministic scripts, or absolute language only when correctness, safety, permissions, or a genuinely fragile workflow requires them.



For open-ended work, describe the outcome and relevant decision criteria. For workflows with a preferred shape, offer useful examples or configurable scripts. Reserve fixed sequences and narrow parameters for operations where deviation would cause a concrete problem. Preserve non-obvious operational invariants, distinguish actual requirements from optional recommendations or local conventions, and avoid restating policies already enforced elsewhere.



**Keep discovery cheap and precise.** Skill names and descriptions are available b<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1998" />es, writing instructions, and validating the result. A narrow update to an existing skill may require only a focused edit and validation.



Ask clarifying questions only when the missing information matters and cannot be reasonably inferred. Respect a user-specified location; otherwise create discoverable skills in `$CODEX_HOME/skills`, or `~/.codex/skills` when `CODEX_HOME` is unset.



Keep automatic skill selection enabled unless the user explicitly requests an explicit-only skill. When the intended invocation mode is genuinely unclear and matters to the requested workflow, ask whether the user wants normal automatic discovery or explicit-only invocation; otherwise preserve the default. Do not infer explicit-only invocation from sensitive operations or required approvals: keep the skill discoverable and require authorization immediately before the actual mutation. Preserve an existing skill's invocation policy unless the user asks to change it.



For a new or substantially revised skill, consider the actual requests it should handle and which reusable resources would improve those tasks:



- A repeated PDF transformation may justify a `scripts/rotate_pdf.py` helper.

- An application-building workflow may benefit from an `assets/frontend-template/` starter.

- A data-analysis skill may need a `references/schema.md` guide to avoid rediscovering table relationships.



Create those resources only when their concrete benefit justifies them. If the user has already explained the task clearly, proceed without requesting additional examples.



### Naming



- Use lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens.

- Keep names under 64 characters and prefer short action-oriented names.

- Namespace by tool or domain when doing so improves discovery.

- Name the skill folder after the skill.



### Initialize a New Skill

"""Register a case only after independent quality, boundaries and native reopening pass."""

import argparse,datetime,hashlib,json

from pathlib import Path



def read(path):return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))

def quality(q,target,label):

    if not q.get('mesh_check_completed') or not float(q['minimum_orthogonal_quality'])>target or not float(q['minimum_cell_volume_m3'])>0:

        raise ValueError(label+' fails the independent quality/positive-volume gate')



def symmetry(audit,label):

    if not audit.get('passed') or audit.get('issues') or not audit.get('boundaries'):raise ValueError(label+' symmetry audit failed')

    for b in audit['boundaries']:

        if b['type']!=7 or b['maximum_plane_distance_m']>1e-9 or b['maximum_face_plane_distance_m']>1e-9:

            raise ValueError(label+' symmetry type or plane geometry failed')

    if audit['relative_area_error']>.001:raise ValueError(label+' symmetry area failed')



def verify(result,reopening,receipt=None):

    target=max(.07,float(result.get('target',.07)))

    if not result.get('status','').startswith('PASS'):raise ValueError('Result is not validated')

    quality(result['fluent'],target,'Candidate');quality(result['saved_case_recheck'],target,'Saved case')

    quality(reopening['quality'],target,'Reopened native project')

    native=float(result['meshing']['after']['OrthogonalAngle']['minimum'])

    if native<=target:raise ValueError('Native mesh quality failed')

    symmetry(result['symmetry_audit'],'Candidate');symmetry(reopening['symmetry'],'Reopened native')

    for label,audit in [('geometry',result['geometry_audit']),('symmetry',result['symmetry_audit']),

                        ('reopened geometry',reopening['geometry']),('reopened symmetry',reopening['symmetry'])]:

Under-Relaxation of the Energy Equation

When you use the pressure-based solver, Ansys Fluent under-relaxes the energy equation using the under-relaxation parameter defined by you in the Solution Controls task page, as described in Setting Under-Relaxation Factors.

Image Solution → Image  Controls

If you are using the non-adiabatic non-premixed combustion model, you will set the energy under-relaxation factor as usual but you will also set an under-relaxation factor for temperature, as described below.

Ansys Fluent uses a default under-relaxation factor of 1.0 for the energy equation, regardless of the form in which it is solved (temperature or enthalpy). In problems where the energy field impacts the fluid flow (via temperature-dependent properties or buoyancy) you should use a lower value for the under-relaxation factor, in the range of 0.8–1.0. In problems where the flow field is decoupled from the temperature field (no temperature-dependent properties or buoyancy forces), you can usually retain the default value of 1.0.

#### 16.2.2.2. Under-Relaxation of Temperature When the Enthalpy Equation is Solved

When the enthalpy form of the energy equation is solved (that is, when you are using the non-adiabatic non-premixed combustion model), Ansys Fluent also under-relaxes the te<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="8281" />d.

## 3.21. Mesh-to-Mesh Solution Interpolation

Ansys Fluent can interpolate solution data for a given geometry from one mesh to another, allowing you to compute a solution using one mesh (for example, hexahedral) and then change to another mesh (for example, hybrid) and continue the calculation using the first solution as a starting point.

Important:  Ansys Fluent does zeroth-order interpolation for interpolating the solution data from one mesh to another.

For additional information, see the following sections:

  * 3.21.1. Performing Mesh-to-Mesh Solution Interpolation
  * 3.21.2. Format of the Interpolation File

### 3.21.1. Performing Mesh-to-Mesh Solution Interpolation

The procedure for mesh-to-mesh solution interpolation is as follows:

  1. Set up the model and calculate a solution on the initial mesh.

  2. Write an interpolation file for the solution data to be interpolated onto the new mesh, using the Interpolate Data dialog box (Figure 3.17: The Interpolate Data Dialog Box).

Image File → Interpolate...

Figure 3.17: The Interpolate Data Dialog Box

Image: The Interpolate Data Dialog Box

L13: ## 6.12. Modifying the Mesh
L14: 
L15: There are several ways in which you can modify or manipulate the mesh after it has been read into Ansys Fluent. You can scale, translate, or rotate the mesh / zones, copy, merge, or separate zones, create or decouple periodic zones, fuse boundaries, and smooth and swap faces. Methods for partitioning meshes to be used in a parallel solver are discussed in cite5†Mesh Partitioning and Load Balancing .
L16: 
L17: * * *
L18: Important:  Whenever you modify the mesh, you should be sure to save a new case file (and a data file, if data exists). If you have old data files that you would like to be able to read in again, be sure to retain the original case file as well, as the data in the old data files may not correspond to the new case file.
L19: 
L20: * * *
L21: 
L22: Information about mesh manipulation is provided in the following sections:
L23:   * ci<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3031" />ration Based on Region L161: You can also separate cell zones based on the marks stored in cell registers. You can mark cells using any of the methods discussed in cite33†Using Cell Registers (for example, you can mark cells with a certain field value range or cells inside or outside a specified region). When you specify which register is to be used for the separation of the cell zone, cells that are marked will be placed into a new cell zone.

L172: * * *
L173: Important:  If you are planning to separate cell zones, you should do so before performing any adaptions using the hanging node adaption method. Cell zones that contain hanging nodes cannot be separated.

"""Optimize tetrahedra by moving only nodes absent from every CAD/interzone surface."""

from pathlib import Path

import json,sys

import numpy as np,h5py

from scipy.sparse import coo_matrix

from scipy.optimize import minimize



def score(p):

    a=p[:,1:]-p[:,0,None,:]

    det=np.linalg.det(a);vol=abs(det)/6

    # Circumsphere center relative to the first vertex, in mesh-local units.

    try:c=np.linalg.solve(2*a,np.sum(a*a,axis=2))

    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:

        c=np.einsum('nij,nj->ni',np.linalg.pinv(2*a),np.sum(a*a,axis=2))

    rad=np.linalg.norm(c,axis=1)

    q=9*np.sqrt(3)/8*vol/np.maximum(rad**3,1e-300)

    return np.clip(q,0,1),det



def load_case(path,token,extras=False):
